# Constants
board = 'EVAL-AD5791SDZ'
chip = 'AD5791'
# Set your ACE installation path
ace_path = r'C:\Program Files (x86)\Analog Devices\ACE'

resolution = 16
if (chip == 'AD5791') or (chip == 'AD5790'):
    resolution = 20
else:
    if(chip == 'AD5781') or (chip == 'AD5780'):
        resolution = 18

code_min =  0
code_max =  2**resolution-1

v_min = -10
v_max = 10

code_array = np.arange(code_min, code_max + 1)
N = len(code_array)
assert(N == 2**resolution)
v_array = np.linspace(v_min, v_max, N)

11/17:
def getchar():
    # Returns a single character from standard input
    import os
    ch = ''
    if os.name == 'nt': # how it works on windows
        import msvcrt
        ch = msvcrt.getch()
    else:
        import tty, termios, sys
        fd = sys.stdin.fileno()
        old_settings = termios.tcgetattr(fd)
        try:
            tty.setraw(sys.stdin.fileno())
            ch = sys.stdin.read(1)
        finally:
            termios.tcsetattr(fd, termios.TCSADRAIN, old_settings)
    if ord(ch) == 3: quit() # handle ctrl+C
    return ch

while 1:
    ch = getchar()
    print 'You pressed %c (%i)' % (ch, ord(ch))

        # Do something else here

#!/usr/bin/python3

import sys
import time
import redpitaya_scpi as scpi




def code_to_tuple(c: int, word_length=8, nb_of_words=3) -> tuple:
    '''Converts a code (integer) to a tuple of binary words
    '''
    try:
        assert isinstance(c, int)
        assert 0 <= c < 2**(nb_of_words*word_length)
    except:
        raise
    else:
        return tuple([ ( c & (2**word_length-1 << i*word_length) ) >> i*word_length for i in range(nb_of_words)[::-1] ])

def tuple_to_code(t: tuple, word_length=8) -> int:
    '''Converts a tuple of words to a code (integer)
    '''
    try:
        assert isinstance(t, tuple)
        for _ in t:
            assert isinstance(_, int)
            assert 0 <= _ < 2**word_length
    except:
        raise
    else:
        return sum([ x << (word_length*i) for i,x in enumerate(t[::-1]) ])

def tuple_to_hexstr(t: tuple) -> str:
    '''Converts a tuple of words to a string of 3 hex numbers like "0xff,0xff,0xff"
    '''
    return ",".join(['#H{0:02X}'.format(_) for _ in t])

def hexstr_to_tuple(s: str) -> tuple:
    '''Converts a string of 3 hex numbers like "{0xff,0xff,0xff}" to a tuple of binary words
    '''
    return tuple([ int(x,16) for x in h.strip('{}').split(',') ])

def intstr_to_tuple(s: str) -> tuple:
    '''Converts a string of 3 integer numbers like "{255,255,255}" to a tuple of binary words
    '''
    return tuple([ int(_) for _ in s.strip('{}').split(',') ])


def code_to_volt(code: int, Vrefp=10.0, Vrefn=-10.0, nbits=20, is_two_complement=True) -> float:
    '''Converts a 20-bit code to the corresponding voltage, using actual voltages on pins Vrefp and Vrefn of DAC
    '''
    if is_two_complement:
        code = code ^ (1 << (nbits-1))
    try:
        assert isinstance(code, int)
        assert 0 <= code < 2**nbits
        voltage = (Vrefp - Vrefn) * float(code) / float(2**nbits - 1) + Vrefn
        assert Vrefn <= voltage <= Vrefp
    except:
        raise
    else:
        return voltage 

def volt_to_code(voltage: float, Vrefp=10.0, Vrefn=-10.0, nbits=20, is_two_complement=True) -> int:
    '''Converts a desired voltage value to a 20-bit code used by the DAC,
    considering actual voltages on pins Vrefp and Vrefn
    '''
    try:
        if isinstance(voltage, int):
            voltage = float(voltage)
        assert isinstance(voltage, float)
        assert Vrefn <= voltage <= Vrefp
        code = int( (voltage - Vrefn) * float(2**nbits - 1) / (Vrefp - Vrefn) )
        assert 0 <= code < 2**nbits
    except:
        raise
    else:
        if is_two_complement:
            code = code ^ (1 << (nbits-1))
        return code

def pprint_code(code):
    return ("{0:024b}".format(code)[0:1], "{0:024b}".format(code)[1:4], "{0:024b}".format(code)[4:8], "{0:024b}".format(code)[8:16], "{0:024b}".format(code)[16:24])



NOP     =     0 << 20   # 0b000000000000000000000000 : NOP value, "no operation", used to read only from SPI
REG_DAC =     1 << 20   # 0b000100000000000000000000
REG_CTL =     2 << 20   # 0b001000000000000000000000
REG_CLR =     3 << 20   # 0b001100000000000000000000
REG_SFT =     4 << 20   # 0b010000000000000000000000

W =          0 << 23    # 0b000000000000000000000000
R =          1 << 23    # 0b100000000000000000000000

# bit masks
MASK_RW  =   0b100000000000000000000000
MASK_REG  =  0b011100000000000000000000
MASK_DATA  = 0b000011111111111111111111

# DAC register
data_min =   0b0 << 0   # 0b000000000000000000000000
data_max =  (0b1 << 20) - 1 # 0b000011111111111111111111
data_zero = data_max >> 1
assert data_zero == data_max // 2
data_default = 0b11 << 18   # 5 V

# Soft register
BIT_LDAC   = 1 << 0 # 0b000000000000000000000001
BIT_CLR    = 1 << 1 # 0b000000000000000000000010
BIT_RESET  = 1 << 2 # 0b000000000000000000000100

 # Control register
BIT_RBUF   = 1 << 1 # 0b000000000000000000000010
BIT_OPGND  = 1 << 2 # 0b000000000000000000000100
BIT_DACTRI = 1 << 3 # 0b000000000000000000001000
BIT_BIN2SC = 1 << 4 # 0b000000000000000000010000
BIT_SDODIS = 1 << 5 # 0b000000000000000000100000
BIT_LINCOMP= 0b1111 << 6# 0b000000000000001111000000 # 0b0000 for -10/+10V range linear compensation


# useful codes
write_middle =      W | REG_DAC | data_zero
write_default =     W | REG_DAC | data_default
read_dac =          R | REG_DAC

prepare_for_write = W | REG_CTL | 0*BIT_LINCOMP | 0*BIT_SDODIS | 0*BIT_BIN2SC | 0*BIT_DACTRI | 0*BIT_OPGND | 1*BIT_RBUF
read_ctrl =         R | REG_CTL

set_clr =           W | REG_CLR | data_default
read_clr =          R | REG_CLR

reset =             W | REG_SFT | BIT_RESET
clear =             W | REG_SFT | BIT_CLR
update_output =     W | REG_SFT | BIT_LDAC
    
    
    
class DAC(object):

    def __init__(self, ip='172.16.10.75', port="5000", spi_speed=100e3):
        self.rp_s = scpi.scpi(ip, port)
        messages_init = [
            'SPI:INIT:DEV "/dev/spidev1.0"',
            'SPI:SET:DEF',
            'SPI:SET:GET',
            'SPI:SET:MODE LIST',
            'SPI:SET:SPEED {0:d}'.format(int(spi_speed)),
            'SPI:SET:WORD 8',
            'SPI:SET:SET',
        ]

        for msg in messages_init:
            print(msg)
            self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)


        self._reg_dac = self.r_single(R | REG_DAC)
        self._reg_ctl = self.reg_ctl()
        self._reg_clr = self.r_single(R | REG_CLR)
        self._reg_sft = self.r_single(R | REG_SFT)

        self._V = self.V

        self.prepare()


    @property
    def reg_dac(self):
        self._reg_dac = self.r_single(R | REG_DAC)
        return self._reg_dac

    @reg_dac.setter
    def reg_dac(self, c: int):
        assert c < MASK_REG
        self._reg_dac = self.w_single(W | REG_DAC | c)

    @property
    def reg_ctl(self):
        self._reg_ctl = self.r_single(R | REG_CTL)
        return self._reg_ctl

    @reg_ctl.setter
    def reg_ctl(self, c: int):
        assert c < MASK_REG
        self._reg_ctl = self.w_single(W | REG_CTL | c)

    @property
    def reg_clr(self):
        self._reg_clr = self.r_single(R | REG_CLR)
        return self._reg_clr

    @reg_clr.setter
    def reg_clr(self, c: int):
        assert c < MASK_REG
        self._reg_clr = self.w_single(W | REG_CLR | c)

    @property
    def reg_sft(self):
        self._reg_sft = self.r_single(R | REG_SFT)
        return self._reg_sft

    @reg_sft.setter
    def reg_sft(self, c: int):
        assert c < MASK_REG
        self._reg_sft = self.w_single(W | REG_SFT | c)


    @property
    def tristate(self):
        out = self.reg_ctl & BIT_DACTRI
        assert out in [0,1]
        return bool(out)

    @tristate.setter
    def tristate(self, yesno: bool):
        assert out in [True, False]
        out = self.reg_ctl(yesno*BIT_DACTRI)

    @property
    def op_gnd(self):
        out = self.reg_ctl & BIT_OPGND
        assert out in [0,1]
        return bool(out)

    @op_gnd.setter
    def op_gnd(self, yesno: bool):
        assert out in [True, False]
        out = self.reg_ctl(yesno*BIT_OPGND)

    @property
    def ldac(self):
        out = self.reg_sft & BIT_LDAC
        assert out in [0,1]
        return bool(out)

    @tristate.setter
    def ldac(self, yesno: bool):
        assert out in [True, False]
        out = self.reg_sft(yesno*BIT_LDAC)



    def __del__(self):
        msg = 'SPI:RELEASE'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)



    def w_single(self, c: int):
        msg = 'SPI:MSG:CREATE 2'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG0:TX3:RX:CS {0}'.format(tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(c)))
        print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        print(pprint_code(c))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG1:TX3:RX:CS {0}'.format(tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(0)))
        print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        print(pprint_code(0))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:PASS'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG0:RX?'
        print(msg, end='')
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        # print(rxbuf)
        print("\t\t--->\t{0}\t{1}".format(tuple_to_hexstr(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)), pprint_code(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)))))

        msg = 'SPI:MSG1:RX?'
        print(msg, end='')
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        # print(rxbuf)
        print("\t\t--->\t{0}\t{1}".format(tuple_to_hexstr(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)), pprint_code(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)))))

        msg = 'SPI:MSG:DEL'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)




    def r_single(self, c: int):
        msg = 'SPI:MSG:CREATE 2'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG0:TX3:RX:CS {0}'.format(tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(c)))
        print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        print(pprint_code(c))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG1:TX3:RX:CS {0}'.format(tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(0)))
        print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        print(pprint_code(0))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:PASS'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG0:RX?'
        print(msg, end='')
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        print(rxbuf)
        print("\t\t--->\t{0}\t{1}".format(tuple_to_hexstr(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)), pprint_code(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)))))

        msg = 'SPI:MSG1:RX?'
        print(msg, end='')
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        print(rxbuf)
        print("\t\t--->\t{0}\t{1}".format(tuple_to_hexstr(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)), pprint_code(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)))))

        msg = 'SPI:MSG:DEL'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        return tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf))


    @property
    def V(self):
        out = self.reg_dac & MASK_DATA
        return code_to_volt(out)

    @V.setter
    def V(self, v):

        msg = 'SPI:MSG:CREATE 3'
        # print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        c = W | REG_DAC | volt_to_code(v)
        msg = 'SPI:MSG0:TX3:RX:CS {0}'.format(tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(c)))
        # print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        # print(pprint_code(c))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        c = R | REG_DAC
        msg = 'SPI:MSG1:TX3:RX:CS {0}'.format(tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(c)))
        # print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        # print(pprint_code(0))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        c = W | REG_SFT | BIT_LDAC
        msg = 'SPI:MSG2:TX3:RX:CS {0}'.format(tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(c)))
        # print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        # print(pprint_code(0))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:PASS'
        # print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG0:RX?'
        # print(msg, end='')
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        # print("\t\t--->\t{0}\t{1}".format(tuple_to_hexstr(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)), pprint_code(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)))))

        msg = 'SPI:MSG1:RX?'
        # print(msg, end='')
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        # print("\t\t--->\t{0}\t{1}".format(tuple_to_hexstr(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)), pprint_code(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)))))

        msg = 'SPI:MSG2:RX?'
        # print(msg, end='')
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        # print("\t\t--->\t{0}\t{1}".format(tuple_to_hexstr(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)), pprint_code(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)))))
        self._V = code_to_volt(tuple_to_code(intstr_to_tuple(rxbuf)) & MASK_DATA)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG:DEL'
        # print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)




    @property
    def clock_freq(self):
        msg = 'SPI:SET:SPEED?'
        print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        rxbuf = self.rp_s.rx_txt()
        print(rxbuf)
        try:
            out = int(rxbuf)
        except:
            raise
        else:
            return out

    @clock_freq.setter
    def clock_freq(self, freq: int):
        try:
            msg = 'SPI:SET:SPEED {0:d}'.format(int(freq))
            print(msg)
            self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)
        except:
            raise
        else:
            pass


    def V_buf(self, v_list, fs=1e3, reg=REG_DAC):
        from time import sleep
        n = len(v_list)

        f_clk_old = self.clock_freq
        self.clock_freq = fs * 2 * 24


        msg = 'SPI:MSG:CREATE {0}'.format(n)
        # print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        c_update = tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(update_output))

        for i in range(n):
            c = W | reg | volt_to_code(v_list[i])
            c = tuple_to_hexstr(code_to_tuple(c))
            msg = 'SPI:MSG{0}:TX6:CS {1},{2}'.format(i, c, c_update)
        # print(msg, end="\t--->\t")
        # print(pprint_code(c))
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:PASS'
        # print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        msg = 'SPI:MSG:DEL'
        # print(msg)
        self.rp_s.tx_txt(msg)

        self.clock_freq = f_clk_old

bpf1 = p.rp.iq2
bpf1.setup(frequency = 150,# center frequency
            bandwidth=10,# the filter quality factor
            acbandwidth = 0,# ac filter to remove pot. input offsets
            phase=0,# nominal phase at center frequency (propagation phase lags not accounted for)
            gain=2.0,# peak gain = +6 dB
            output_direct='out2',
            output_signal='output_direct',
            input='asg0'
            )

20/755:
def is_bit_in_code(c: int, b:int) -> bool:
    out = c & b
    # assert out in [0, b]
    return bool(out)

def is_bit_in_code(c: int, b:int) -> bool:
    print(bin(a))
    print(bin(b))
    out = c & b
    print(bin(out))
    out &= b
    print(bin(out))
    return bool(out)

    print(f"{b,024b}")
    out = c & b
    print(f"{out,024b}")

20/775:
def is_bit_in_code(c: int, b:int) -> bool:
    print(f"{c:024b}")
    print(f"{b:024b}")
    _ = c & b    # test if at least one bit in b is in c
    print(f"{_:024b}")
    return _ == b   # only return True if not only b is in c but also b doesn't contain bits not in c
20/776: is_bit_in_code(BIT_LINCOMP | BIT_SDODIS | BIT_BIN2SC | BIT_DACTRI | BIT_OPGND | BIT_RBUF, BIT_DACTRI | 1)

22/31:
# shortcut for na
na = p.networkanalyzer
na.iq_name = 'iq1'

# setup network analyzer with the right parameters
na.setup(start=1e3,stop=62.5e6,points=1001,rbw=1000, avg=1,
amplitude=0.2,input='iq1',output_direct='off', acbandwidth=0)

#take transfer functions. first: iq1 -> iq1, second iq1->out1->(your cable)->adc1
iq1 = na.curve()
na.setup(input='in1', output_direct='out1')
in1 = na.curve()

# get x-axis for plotting
f = na.frequencies

#plot
from pyrpl.hardware_modules.iir.iir_theory import bodeplot

bodeplot([(f, iq1, "iq1->iq1"), (f, in1, "iq1->out1->in1->iq1")], xlog=True)

from pyrpl import Pyrpl

p = Pyrpl(config='', gui=False, hostname='172.16.10.75')

p.rp.iq0.free()
p.rp.iq1.free()
p.rp.iq2.free()

p.rp.iq1.setup(frequency=150,bandwith=10, phase=30, gain=0.5, output_direct='out2', output_signal='output_direct', input='iq0')
p.rp.iq2.setup(frequency=250,bandwidth=20, phase=60, gain=0.75, output_direct='out2', output_signal='output_direct', input='iq0')

na = p.networkanalyzer
na.iq_name = 'iq0'
na.setup(start_freq=1e1,stop_freq=1e3,points=101,rbw=10, trace_average=1, average_per_point=1,amplitude=1,input='out2',output_direct='out1', acbandwidth=0)
ff = na.frequencies
zz = na.single()

from pyrpl.hardware_modules.iir.iir_theory import bodeplot
b = bodeplot((ff, zz, "iq0->out1->in1->iq0"), xlog=True)

na = p.networkanalyzer
na.iq_name = 'iq2'
is_log = True
is_HV = True
N = 5
M = 3
import numpy as np
amplitudes = np.linspace(0,10,M)
zzz = np.nan((N,M))
na.sleeptimes=0.5

for m in range(M):
    na.setup(   start_freq=10,
                stop_freq=1000,
                points=N,
                logscale=is_log,
                rbw=1,
                q_factor_min=50,
                auto_bandwidth=False,
                trace_average=1,
                average_per_point=1,
                amplitude=amplitudes[m],
                input='in1',
                output_direct='out1',
                acbandwidth=0)

    %time zzz[:,m] = na.single() * 20.*is_HV

ff = na.frequencies


from pyrpl.hardware_modules.iir.iir_theory import bodeplot
bodeplot([ (ff, zzz[:,m], f"{amplitude[m]:.1f} V") for m in range(M)], xlog=is_log)

amplitudes = np.linspace(0,1,M+1)[1:]
zzz = np.ones((N,M), dtype=np.complex128)*np.nan
na.sleeptimes=1.0

for m in range(M):
    na.setup(   start_freq=100,
                stop_freq=200,
                points=N,
                logscale=is_log,
                rbw=1,
                q_factor_min=50,
                auto_bandwidth=False,
                trace_average=1,
                average_per_point=1,
                amplitude=amplitudes[m],
                input='in1',
                output_direct='out1',
                acbandwidth=0)

    %time zzz[:,m] = na.single() * 20.*is_HV
    np.save("zzz", zzz)

ff = na.frequencies


from pyrpl.hardware_modules.iir.iir_theory import bodeplot
bodeplot([ (ff, zzz[:,m], f"{amplitudes[m]:.1f} V") for m in range(M)], xlog=is_log)

p.rp.asg0.waveform = 'sin'

fext = 50.0
q = 1.56

gen.amplitude = 0.9
gen.offset = 0.45
gen.start_phase = 30.0

gen = p.rp.asg0

plt.plot(gen.data, '.', '.data before .waveform')

gen.waveform = 'sin'

plt.plot(gen.data, '.', '.data after .waveform')

gen.frequency = f0
gen.amplitude = 0.9
gen.offset = 0.45
gen.start_phase = 30.0

# gen.trigger_source = 'off'

gen.output_direct = 'out2'

# gen.cycles_per_burst = 0
gen.bursts = 0
# gen.periodic = False


gen.data = x.copy()

# gen.data = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[0:W] = np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[k:k+W] = -np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()

# np.put(gen.data, range(W), 1.0)
# np.put(gen.data, range(k,k+W), -1.0)
gen.cycles_per_burst = 1
sleep(0.1)
# gen.trigger_source = 'immediately'
gen.trigger_source = 'ext_positive_edge'

import numpy as np
from time import sleep
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from pyrpl import Pyrpl

p = Pyrpl(hostname='172.16.10.75', config='', gui=False)

N = 2**14
fext = 50.0

q = 1.56
f0 = q*fext

k = int(np.round(N/2 * f0/fext))
W = 32
assert k+W < N

x = np.zeros(N)# , dtype='float64')
x[0:W] = 1.0
x[k:k+W] = -1.0

plt.plot(x, '-', alpha=0.5, label='array created')

gen = p.rp.asg0
gen.trigger_source = 'off'
gen.frequency = f0
gen.amplitude = 1.0
gen.offset = 0.0
gen.start_phase = 0.0
gen.output_direct = 'out2'
gen.bursts = 0
gen.cycles_per_burst = 1
# gen.periodic = False

gen.waveform = 'sin'


def unwrap(X:int) -> float:
    X[ X >= 2**13] = (X - 2**14 ) / 2**14
    X[ X <  2**13] = X / 2**14
    return X


plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .waveform')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after .waveform')



gen.data = x.copy()

# gen.data = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[0:W] = np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[k:k+W] = -np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()

# np.put(gen.data, range(W), 1.0)
# np.put(gen.data, range(k,k+W), -1.0)

gen.trigger_source = 'ext_positive_edge'
# gen.trigger_source = 'immediately'
# gen.cycles_per_burst = 0

plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .data = x.copy()')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after .data = x.copy()')

plt.ylabel('amplitude')

import numpy as np
from time import sleep
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from pyrpl import Pyrpl

p = Pyrpl(hostname='172.16.10.75', config='', gui=False)

N = 2**14
fext = 50.0

q = 1.56
f0 = q*fext

k = int(np.round(N/2 * f0/fext))
W = 32
assert k+W < N

x = np.zeros(N)# , dtype='float64')
x[0:W] = 1.0
x[k:k+W] = -1.0

plt.plot(x, '-', alpha=0.5, label='array created')

gen = p.rp.asg0
gen.trigger_source = 'off'
gen.frequency = f0
gen.amplitude = 1.0
gen.offset = 0.0
gen.start_phase = 0.0
gen.output_direct = 'out2'
gen.bursts = 0
gen.cycles_per_burst = 1
# gen.periodic = False

gen.waveform = 'sin'


def unwrap(X:int) -> float:
    x = np.zeros_like(X, dtype=float)
    x[ X >= 2**13 ] = (float(X[ X >= 2**13 ]) - 2.0**14 ) / 2.0**14
    x[ X <  2**13 ] = float(X[ X < 2**13 ]) / 2.0**14
    return x


plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .waveform')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after .waveform')



gen.data = x.copy()

# gen.data = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[0:W] = np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[k:k+W] = -np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()

# np.put(gen.data, range(W), 1.0)
# np.put(gen.data, range(k,k+W), -1.0)

gen.trigger_source = 'ext_positive_edge'
# gen.trigger_source = 'immediately'
# gen.cycles_per_burst = 0

plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .data = x.copy()')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after .data = x.copy()')
plt.legend()
plt.xlabel('index')
plt.ylabel('amplitude')
plt.savefig('test.pdf')

import numpy as np
from time import sleep
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from pyrpl import Pyrpl

p = Pyrpl(hostname='172.16.10.75', config='', gui=False)

N = 2**14
fext = 50.0

q = 1.56
f0 = q*fext

k = int(np.round(N/2 * f0/fext))
W = 32
assert k+W < N

x = np.zeros(N)# , dtype='float64')
x[0:W] = 1.0
x[k:k+W] = -1.0

plt.plot(x, '-', alpha=0.5, label='array created')

gen = p.rp.asg0
gen.trigger_source = 'off'
gen.frequency = f0
gen.amplitude = 1.0
gen.offset = 0.0
gen.start_phase = 0.0
gen.output_direct = 'out2'
gen.bursts = 0
gen.cycles_per_burst = 1
# gen.periodic = False

gen.waveform = 'sin'


def unwrap(X:int) -> float:
    x = np.zeros_like(X, dtype=float)
    x[:] = X[:]
    x[ X >= 2**13 ] = (x[ X >= 2**13 ] - 2.0**14 ) / 2.0**14
    x[ X <  2**13 ] = x[ X < 2**13 ] / 2.0**14
    return x


plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .waveform')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after .waveform')



gen.data = x.copy()

# gen.data = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[0:W] = np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[k:k+W] = -np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()

# np.put(gen.data, range(W), 1.0)
# np.put(gen.data, range(k,k+W), -1.0)

gen.trigger_source = 'ext_positive_edge'
# gen.trigger_source = 'immediately'
# gen.cycles_per_burst = 0

plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .data = x.copy()')

plt.ylabel('amplitude')

W = 32
assert k+W < N

x = np.zeros(N)# , dtype='float64')
x[0:W] = 1.0
x[k:k+W] = -1.0

plt.plot(x, '-', alpha=0.5, label='array created')

gen = p.rp.asg0
gen.trigger_source = 'off'
gen.frequency = f0
gen.amplitude = 1.0
gen.offset = 0.0
gen.start_phase = 0.0
gen.output_direct = 'out2'
gen.bursts = 0
gen.cycles_per_burst = 1
# gen.periodic = False

gen.waveform = 'sin'


def unwrap(X:int) -> float:
    x = np.zeros_like(X, dtype=float)
    x[:] = X[:]
    x[ X >= 2**13 ] = (x[ X >= 2**13 ] - 2.0**14 ) / 2.0**13
    x[ X <  2**13 ] = x[ X < 2**13 ] / 2.0**13
    return x


plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .waveform')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after .waveform')



gen.data = x.copy()

# gen.data = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[0:W] = np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[k:k+W] = -np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()

# np.put(gen.data, range(W), 1.0)
# np.put(gen.data, range(k,k+W), -1.0)

gen.trigger_source = 'ext_positive_edge'
# gen.trigger_source = 'immediately'
# gen.cycles_per_burst = 0

plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after .data = x.copy()')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after .data = x.copy()')
plt.legend()
plt.xlabel('index')
plt.ylabel('amplitude')
plt.savefig('test.pdf')
plt.close()

gen.start_phase = 180.0
gen.output_direct = 'out2'
# gen.bursts = 0
gen.cycles_per_burst = 1
# gen.periodic = False


def unwrap(X:int) -> float:
    x = np.zeros_like(X, dtype=float)
    x[:] = X[:]
    x[ X >= 2**13 ] = (x[ X >= 2**13 ] - 2.0**14 ) / 2.0**13
    x[ X <  2**13 ] = x[ X < 2**13 ] / 2.0**13
    return x


plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata before set')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data before set')



# gen.data[:] = x[:]
gen.data = x

# gen.data = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[0:W] = np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[k:k+W] = -np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()

# np.put(gen.data, range(W), 1.0)
# np.put(gen.data, range(k,k+W), -1.0)

# gen.trigger_source = 'immediately'
# gen.cycles_per_burst = 0

plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after set')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after set')
plt.legend()
plt.xlabel('index')
plt.ylabel('amplitude')
plt.savefig('test.pdf')

30/149:
import numpy as np
from time import sleep
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from pyrpl import Pyrpl

p = Pyrpl(hostname='172.16.10.75', config='', gui=False)

fext = 50.0

q = 1.56
f0 = q*fext

N = 2**14
W = 32    # pulse width
k = int(np.round(N/2 * f0/fext))
k0 = 1 # a >0 delay must be set because output is set to 1st value of data array while waiting for trigger
assert k0 + k + W < N
x = np.zeros(N)# , dtype='float64')
x[k0:W] = 1.0
x[k0 + k:k0 + k + W] = -1.0

plt.plot(x, '-', alpha=0.5, label='array created')

gen = p.rp.asg0
gen.trigger_source = 'ext_postive_edge'
gen.waveform = 'dc'
gen.frequency = f0
gen.amplitude = 1.0
gen.offset = 0.0
gen.start_phase = 0.0
gen.output_direct = 'out2'
# gen.bursts = 0
gen.cycles_per_burst = 1
# gen.periodic = False


def unwrap(X:int) -> float:
    x = np.zeros_like(X, dtype=float)
    x[:] = X[:]
    x[ X >= 2**13 ] = (x[ X >= 2**13 ] - 2.0**14 ) / 2.0**13
    x[ X <  2**13 ] = x[ X < 2**13 ] / 2.0**13
    return x


plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata before set')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data before set')



# gen.data[:] = x[:]
gen.data = x

# gen.data = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[0:W] = np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()
# gen.data[k:k+W] = -np.ones(W, dtype='float64').copy()

# np.put(gen.data, range(W), 1.0)
# np.put(gen.data, range(k,k+W), -1.0)

# gen.trigger_source = 'immediately'
# gen.cycles_per_burst = 0

plt.plot(unwrap(gen._writtendata), '-', alpha=0.5, label='._writtendata after set')
plt.plot(gen.data, '-', alpha=0.5, label='.data after set')
plt.legend()
plt.xlabel('index')
plt.ylabel('amplitude')
plt.savefig('test.pdf')
plt.close()

config_init_asg =   {
                    'ff_frequency': 100.0,
                    'ff_amplitude': 0.5,
                    'ff_offset': 0.0,
                    'ff_phase': 0.0,
                    'ff_out': 'out2',
                    }

# config_init_bpf =   {
#                     'f0': 50,
#                     'g0': 3,
#                     'p0': 0,
#                     'f1': 150,
#                     'g1': 3,
#                     'p1': 120,
#                     'f2': 250,
#                     'g2': 3,
#                     'p2': 240,
#                     }

config_init_bpf0 =  {
                    'f': 50,
                    'g': 3,
                    'p': 0,
                    }
config_init_bpf1 =  {
                    'f': 150,
                    'g': 3,
                    'p': 120,
                    }
config_init_bpf2 =  {
                    'f': 250,
                    'g': 3,
                    'p': 240,
                    }

config_init_bpf =   {
                    'config_bpf0': config_init_bpf0,
                    'config_bpf1': config_init_bpf1,
                    'config_bpf2': config_init_bpf2
                    }

config_init_prefilter = {
                        'ff_pre_lpf': 4858, # low-pass filter frequency (Hz) applied on generated signal before selective band-pass filters
                        'ff_pre_hpf': 2429, # high-pass filter frequency (Hz) applied on generated signal before selective band-pass filters
                        'ff_pre_gain': 2,   # gain applied on ff signal _after LP and HP filters_, before selective band-pass filters
                        }

config_init_ff = {**config_init_asg, **config_init_bpf, **config_init_prefilter}


config_init_fb =    {
                    'fb_gain_p': 1,
                    'fb_gain_i': 0.1,
                    'fb_in': 'in1',
                    'fb_out': 'out1',
                    }

class RPCurrentShunt(object):

    
    def __init__(self, ip='172.16.10.75', yaml_file=''): #yaml_file='/Users/labo/Documents/python/test.yml'):
        from pyrpl import Pyrpl
        self.yaml_file = yaml_file
        self.p = Pyrpl(hostname=ip, config=self.yaml_file, gui=False)
        self.feedforward_init(**config_init_ff)
        # self.feedback_init(**config_init_fb)

    def __del__(self):
        del self.p.rp
        del self.p


    def ff_dds_function(fext=49.98,  q=1.56, W=1024):
        N = 2**14
        f0 = q*fext
        k = int(np.round(N/2 * f0/fext))
        k0 = 1 # a >0 delay must be set because output is set to 1st value of data array while waiting for trigger
        assert k0 + k + W < N
        x = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64')
        x[k0:k0 + W] = 1.0
        x[k0 + k:k0 + k + W] = -1.0
        return x
    
    def feedforward_init(   self,
                            ff_frequency_ext=49.98,
                            ff_amplitude=1.0,
                            ff_pre_gain=0,
                            ff_pre_lpf=0,
                            ff_pre_hpf=0,
                            ff_phase=0,
                            ff_out='off',
                            config_bpf0=config_init_bpf0,
                            config_bpf1=config_init_bpf1,
                            config_bpf2=config_init_bpf2,
                        ):

        self.ff_asg  = self.p.rp.asg0
        self.ff_bpf0 = self.p.rp.iq0
        self.ff_bpf1 = self.p.rp.iq1
        self.ff_bpf2 = self.p.rp.iq2
        # self.ff_prefilter = self.p.rp.pid0

        q = 1.56

        self.ff_asg.setup(waveform='dc',
                            frequency=q*ff_frequency_ext,
                            amplitude=ff_amplitude,
                            offset=0.0,
                            start_phase=ff_phase,
                            trigger_source='ext_positive_edge',
                            output_direct='off',
                            cycles_per_burst=1
                        )
        
        
        self.ff_asg.data = ff_dds_function()
        # self.ff_asg.frequency = q*49.98
        # self.ff_asg.amplitude = 1.0
        # self.ff_asg.offset = 0.0
        
        # self.ff_prefilter.setup(    p=ff_pre_gain,
        #                             i=0,
        #                             input=self.ff_asg.name,
        #                             output_direct='off',
        #                             inputfilter=[ ff_pre_lpf, -ff_pre_hpf, 0, 0 ]
        #                         )

        for bpf,cfg in zip( [ self.ff_bpf0, self.ff_bpf1, self.ff_bpf2 ],
                            [ config_bpf0, config_bpf1, config_bpf2 ]
                        ):
            bpf.setup(frequency=cfg['f'],# center frequency
                        bandwidth=cfg['f']/50.,# the filter quality factor
                        acbandwidth=10.,# ac filter to remove pot. input offsets
                        phase=cfg['p'],# nominal phase at center frequency (propagation phase lags not accounted for)
                        gain=cfg['g'],# peak gain=+6 dB
                        output_direct=ff_out,
                        output_signal='output_direct',
                        input=self.ff_prefilter.name
                        )


    def feedback_init(  self, 
                        fb_gain_p=1,
                        fb_gain_i=0,
                        fb_in='in1',
                        fb_out='out1',
                        fb_lpf=0,
                        fb_hpf=0
                    ):

        self.ff_pid1 = self.p.rp.pid1
        self.ff_pid1.setup( p=fb_gain_p,
                            i=fb_gain_i,
                            input=fb_in,
                            output_direct=fb_out,
                            inputfilter=[ fb_lpff, -fb_hpf, 0, 0 ]
                        )
        

    @property
    def ff0_f(self):
        return self.ff_bpf0
    @ff0_f.setter
    def ff0_f(self, f):
        self.ff_bpf0.frequency = f

def ff_dds_function(fext=49.98,  q=1.56, W=2048):
    import numpy as np
    N = 2**14
    f0 = q*fext
    k = int(np.round(N/2 * f0/fext))
    k0 = 1 # a >0 delay must be set because output is set to 1st value of data array while waiting for trigger
    assert k0 + k + W < N
    x = np.zeros(N, dtype='float64')
    x[k0:k0 + W] = 1.0
    x[k0 + k:k0 + k + W] = -1.0
    return x

class RPCurrentShunt(object):

    
    def __init__(self, ip='172.16.10.75', yaml_file=''): #yaml_file='/Users/labo/Documents/python/test.yml'):
        from pyrpl import Pyrpl
        self.yaml_file = yaml_file
        self.p = Pyrpl(hostname=ip, config=self.yaml_file, gui=False)
        self.feedforward_init(**config_init_ff)
        # self.feedback_init(**config_init_fb)

    def __del__(self):
        del self.p.rp
        del self.p


    
    def feedforward_init(   self,
                            ff_frequency_ext=49.98,
                            ff_amplitude=1.0,
                            ff_pre_gain=0,
                            ff_pre_lpf=0,
                            ff_pre_hpf=0,
                            ff_phase=0,
                            ff_out='off',
                            config_bpf0=config_init_bpf0,
                            config_bpf1=config_init_bpf1,
                            config_bpf2=config_init_bpf2,
                        ):

        self.ff_asg  = self.p.rp.asg0
        self.ff_bpf0 = self.p.rp.iq0
        self.ff_bpf1 = self.p.rp.iq1
        self.ff_bpf2 = self.p.rp.iq2
        # self.ff_prefilter = self.p.rp.pid0

        q = 1.56

        self.ff_asg.setup(waveform='dc',
                            frequency=q*ff_frequency_ext,
                            amplitude=ff_amplitude,
                            offset=0.0,
                            start_phase=ff_phase,
                            trigger_source='ext_positive_edge',
                            output_direct='off',
                            cycles_per_burst=1
                        )
        
        
        self.ff_asg.data = ff_dds_function()
        # self.ff_asg.frequency = q*49.98
        # self.ff_asg.amplitude = 1.0
        # self.ff_asg.offset = 0.0
        
        # self.ff_prefilter.setup(    p=ff_pre_gain,
        #                             i=0,
        #                             input=self.ff_asg.name,
        #                             output_direct='off',
        #                             inputfilter=[ ff_pre_lpf, -ff_pre_hpf, 0, 0 ]
        #                         )

        for bpf,cfg in zip( [ self.ff_bpf0, self.ff_bpf1, self.ff_bpf2 ],
                            [ config_bpf0, config_bpf1, config_bpf2 ]
                        ):
            bpf.setup(frequency=cfg['f'],# center frequency
                        bandwidth=cfg['f']/50.,# the filter quality factor
                        acbandwidth=10.,# ac filter to remove pot. input offsets
                        phase=cfg['p'],# nominal phase at center frequency (propagation phase lags not accounted for)
                        gain=cfg['g'],# peak gain=+6 dB
                        output_direct=ff_out,
                        output_signal='output_direct',
                        input=self.ff_asg.name
                        )


    def feedback_init(  self, 
                        fb_gain_p=1,
                        fb_gain_i=0,
                        fb_in='in1',
                        fb_out='out1',
                        fb_lpf=0,
                        fb_hpf=0
                    ):

                            i=fb_gain_i,
                            input=fb_in,
                            output_direct=fb_out,
                            inputfilter=[ fb_lpf, -fb_hpf, 0, 0 ]
                        )

        return self.ff_bpf0

31/256:
def plot_rp(scope, axes, ch1='in1', ch2='out1', label='', rbw=1):
    scope.input1 = ch1
    scope.input2 = ch2
    sleep(2)
    x1, x2 = scope.single()
    t = scope.times
    y1 =  spectrum(x1, rbw)
    y2 =  spectrum(x2, rbw)
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(t), np.mean(np.diff(t)))
    ax[0,0].plot(t*1e3, x1,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input1 + ') ' + label)
    ax[0,1].plot(t*1e3, x2,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input2 + ') ' + label)
    ax[1,0].loglog(f, y1,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input1 + ') ' + label)
    ax[1,1].loglog(f, y2,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input2 + ') ' + label)
31/257: rbz
31/258: rbw
31/259:
def fullplot_rp(scope, ch1='in1', ch2='out1', label='', rbw=1):
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(24,12), dpi=150)

    plot_rp(scope, axes=ax, ch1=ch1, ch2=ch2, label=label, rbw=rbw)

    ax[0,0].legend()
    ax[0,1].legend()
    ax[1,0].legend()
    ax[1,1].legend()
    ax[0,0].grid(True, linewidth=0.25)
    ax[0,1].grid(True, linewidth=0.25)
    ax[1,0].grid(True, linewidth=0.25, which='both')
    ax[1,1].grid(True, linewidth=0.25, which='both')

    ax[0,0].set_xlabel('time (ms)')
    ax[0,0].set_ylabel('V')
    ax[0,0].set_xlim([-50, 50])
    ax[0,0].set_ylim([-1.1, 1.1])

    ax[0,1].set_xlabel('time (ms)')
    ax[0,1].set_ylabel('V')
    ax[0,1].set_xlim([-50, 50])
    ax[0,1].set_ylim([-1.1, 1.1])

    ax[1,0].set_xlabel('freq (Hz)')
    ax[1,0].set_ylabel(r'V / $\sqrt{{}}$Hz')
    ax[1,0].set_xlim([10, 10e3])

    ax[1,1].set_xlabel('freq (Hz)')
    ax[1,1].set_ylabel(r'V / $\sqrt{{}}$Hz')
    ax[1,1].set_xlim([10, 10e3])

    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig('scope.pdf')
    plt.close()
31/260: fullplot_rp(s, rbw=rbw)
31/261: rbw
31/262:
def fullplot_rp(scope, ch1='in1', ch2='out1', label='', rbw=0.9313225746154784):
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(24,12), dpi=150)

    scope.input1 = ch1
    scope.input2 = ch2
    sleep(2)
    x1, x2 = scope.single()
    t = scope.times
    y1 =  spectrum(x1, rbw)
    y2 =  spectrum(x2, rbw)
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(t), np.mean(np.diff(t)))
    ax[0,0].plot(t*1e3, x1,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input1 + ') ' + label)
    ax[0,1].plot(t*1e3, x2,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input2 + ') ' + label)
    ax[1,0].loglog(f, y1,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input1 + ') ' + label)
    ax[1,1].loglog(f, y2,  '-', linewidth=0.75, alpha=0.75, label='(' + scope.input2 + ') ' + label)

    ax[0,0].legend()
    ax[0,1].legend()
    ax[1,0].legend()
    ax[1,1].legend()
    ax[0,0].grid(True, linewidth=0.25)
    ax[0,1].grid(True, linewidth=0.25)
    ax[1,0].grid(True, linewidth=0.25, which='both')
    ax[1,1].grid(True, linewidth=0.25, which='both')

    ax[0,0].set_xlabel('time (ms)')
    ax[0,0].set_ylabel('V')
    ax[0,0].set_xlim([-50, 50])
    ax[0,0].set_ylim([-1.1, 1.1])

    ax[0,1].set_xlabel('time (ms)')
    ax[0,1].set_ylabel('V')
    ax[0,1].set_xlim([-50, 50])
    ax[0,1].set_ylim([-1.1, 1.1])

    ax[1,0].set_xlabel('freq (Hz)')
    ax[1,0].set_ylabel(r'V / $\sqrt{{}}$Hz')
    ax[1,0].set_xlim([10, 10e3])

    ax[1,1].set_xlabel('freq (Hz)')
    ax[1,1].set_ylabel(r'V / $\sqrt{{}}$Hz')
    ax[1,1].set_xlim([10, 10e3])

    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig('scope.pdf')
    plt.close()

32/29:
def spectrum(x: float, rbw=0.9313225746154784) -> float:
    y = np.fft.rfft(x, norm='ortho')
    y = np.sqrt(np.abs(y)**2 * 2)   # this forumla ensures sum(x**2) == sum(y**2)
    return y/np.sqrt(rbw)           # now a spectral density in V/sqrt(Hz) : ensures sum(x**2) == sum(y**2) * rbw
